{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /etc/claude-code/.claude/skills/manufacturing-failure-reason-codebook-normalization\n\nThis skill should be considered when you need to normalize, standardize, or correct testing engineers' written failure reasons to match the requirements provided in the product codebooks. Common errors in engineer-written reasons include ambiguous descriptions, missing important words, improper personal writing habits, using wrong abbreviations, improper combining multiple reasons into one sentence without clear spacing or in wrong order, writing wrong station names or model, writing typos, improper combining Chinese and English characters, cross-project differences, and taking wrong products' codebook.\n\nSome codes are defined for specific stations and cannot be used by other stations. If entry.stations is not None, the predicted code should only be considered valid when the record station matches one of the stations listed in entry.stations. Otherwise, the code should be rejected. For each record segment, the system evaluates candidate codes defined in the corresponding product codebook and computes an internal matching score for each candidate. You should consider multiple evidence sources to calculate the score to measure how well a candidate code explains the segment, and normalize the score to a stable range [0.0, 1.0]. Evidence can include text evidence from raw_reason_text (e.g., overlap or fuzzy similarity between span_text and codebook text such as standard_label, keywords_examples, or categories), station compatibility, fail_code alignment, test_item alignment, and conflict cues such as mutually exclusive or contradictory signals. After all candidate codes are scored, sort them in descending order. Let c1 be the top candidate with score s1 and c2 be the second candidate with score s2. When multiple candidates fall within a small margin of the best score, the system applies a deterministic tie-break based on record context (e.g., record_id, segment index, station, fail_code, test_item) to avoid always choosing the same code in near-tie cases while keeping outputs reproducible. To provide convincing answers, add station, fail_code, test_item, a short token overlap cue, or a component reference to the rationale.\n\nUNKNOWN handling: UNKNOWN should be decided based on the best match only (i.e., after ranking), not by marking multiple candidates. If the best-match score is low (weak evidence), output pred_code=\"UNKNOWN\" and pred_label=\"\" to give engineering an alert. When strong positive cues exist (e.g., clear component references), UNKNOWN should be less frequent than in generic or noisy segments.\n\nConfidence calibration: confidence ranges from 0.0 to 1.0 and reflects an engineering confidence level (not a probability). Calibrate confidence from match quality so that UNKNOWN predictions are generally less confident than non-UNKNOWN predictions, and confidence values are not nearly constant. Confidence should show distribution-level separation between UNKNOWN and non-UNKNOWN predictions (e.g., means, quantiles, and diversity), and should be weakly aligned with evidence strength; round confidence to 4 decimals.\n\nHere is a pipeline reference\n1) Load test_center_logs.csv into logs_rows and load each product codebook; build valid_code_set, station_scope_map, and CodebookEntry objects.  \n2) For each record, split raw_reason_text into 1–N segments; each segment uses segment_id=<record_id>-S<i> and keeps an exact substring as span_text.  \n3) For each segment, filter candidates by station scope, then compute match score from combined evidence (text evidence, station compatibility, context alignment, and conflict cues).  \n4) Rank candidates by score; if multiple are within a small margin of the best, choose deterministically using a context-dependent tie-break among near-best station-compatible candidates.  \n5) Output exactly one pred_code/pred_label per segment from the product codebook (or UNKNOWN/\"\" when best evidence is weak) and compute confidence by calibrating match quality with sufficient diversity; round to 4 decimals.\n"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"fb0c6a64-58e6-4e96-b15c-e654f526a3e3","uuid":"874a1b42-7cdf-4b93-b7ba-222678adc415","isSynthetic":true}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_vrtx_01Eerz5c9YKtUdpQgw9Si65H","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01NCCWSwKt4NKYy8hhyZz89p","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/app/normalize.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nManufacturing Failure Reason Codebook Normalization System\n\nThis script normalizes raw defect reason texts from testing engineers into\nstandardized codebook format following the skill guidelines.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport csv\nimport json\nimport re\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom typing import List, Dict, Set, Tuple, Optional\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, asdict\nfrom difflib import SequenceMatcher\nimport hashlib\n\n@dataclass\nclass CodebookEntry:\n    \"\"\"Represents a single codebook entry\"\"\"\n    product_id: str\n    code: str\n    standard_label: str\n    category_lv1: str\n    category_lv2: str\n    station_scope: Set[str]\n    keywords_examples: Set[str]\n\n@dataclass\nclass NormalizedSegment:\n    \"\"\"Represents a normalized segment\"\"\"\n    segment_id: str\n    span_text: str\n    pred_code: str\n    pred_label: str\n    confidence: float\n    rationale: str\n\n@dataclass\nclass NormalizedRecord:\n    \"\"\"Represents a normalized record\"\"\"\n    record_id: str\n    product_id: str\n    station: str\n    engineer_id: str\n    raw_reason_text: str\n    normalized: List[NormalizedSegment]\n\nclass CodebookNormalizer:\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.codebooks = {}  # product_id -> list of CodebookEntry\n        self.valid_code_set = {}  # product_id -> set of codes\n        self.station_scope_map = {}  # (product_id, code) -> set of stations\n        self.keyword_index = {}  # product_id -> code -> normalized keywords\n\n    def load_codebooks(self, codebook_paths: Dict[str, str]):\n        \"\"\"Load all product codebooks\"\"\"\n        for product_id, path in codebook_paths.items():\n            entries = []\n            with open(path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n                reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n                for row in reader:\n                    station_scope = set(s.strip() for s in row['station_scope'].split(';'))\n                    keywords = set(s.strip() for s in row['keywords_examples'].split(','))\n\n                    entry = CodebookEntry(\n                        product_id=row['product_id'],\n                        code=row['code'],\n                        standard_label=row['standard_label'],\n                        category_lv1=row['category_lv1'],\n                        category_lv2=row['category_lv2'],\n                        station_scope=station_scope,\n                        keywords_examples=keywords\n                    )\n                    entries.append(entry)\n\n            self.codebooks[product_id] = entries\n            self.valid_code_set[product_id] = {e.code for e in entries}\n\n            # Build keyword index\n            self.keyword_index[product_id] = {}\n            for entry in entries:\n                normalized_keywords = set()\n                for kw in entry.keywords_examples:\n                    normalized_keywords.add(self._normalize_text(kw))\n                self.keyword_index[product_id][entry.code] = normalized_keywords\n\n            # Build station scope map\n            for entry in entries:\n                key = (product_id, entry.code)\n                self.station_scope_map[key] = entry.station_scope\n\n    def _normalize_text(self, text: str) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Normalize text for comparison\"\"\"\n        # Convert to lowercase, remove extra spaces\n        text = text.lower().strip()\n        # Remove punctuation and normalize whitespace\n        text = re.sub(r'[\\s\\-_/,;\\.]+', ' ', text)\n        return text\n\n    def _tokenize(self, text: str) -> List[str]:\n        \"\"\"Tokenize text into meaningful units\"\"\"\n        text = self._normalize_text(text)\n        # Split on whitespace and punctuation\n        tokens = re.findall(r'\\b\\w+\\b', text)\n        return tokens\n\n    def _segment_text(self, raw_text: str) -> List[str]:\n        \"\"\"Split raw_reason_text into logical segments\"\"\"\n        if not raw_text or not raw_text.strip():\n            return []\n\n        # Split on common separators: comma, slash, Chinese full-width punctuation\n        # Patterns: comma, slash, \"and\" (中文\"和\"), semicolon, Chinese period\n        segments = re.split(r'[,/;，；、]|(?<=。)', raw_text)\n\n        # Clean segments: strip whitespace and filter out empty\n        segments = [s.strip() for s in segments if s.strip()]\n\n        # If only one segment, it might contain multiple issues\n        if len(segments) == 1 and len(raw_text) > 50:\n            # Try splitting by English keywords\n            text = raw_text\n            # Look for patterns like \"A, B / C\" or \"A及B\"\n            if ' / ' in text or ' and ' in text.lower():\n                parts = re.split(r'\\s+/\\s+|\\s+and\\s+', text, flags=re.IGNORECASE)\n                if len(parts) > 1:\n                    segments = [p.strip() for p in parts if p.strip()]\n            # Chinese \"及\"\n            elif '及' in text:\n                parts = text.split('及')\n                if len(parts) > 1:\n                    segments = [p.strip() for p in parts if p.strip()]\n\n        return segments if segments else [raw_text]\n\n    def _text_similarity(self, text1: str, text2: str) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Compute similarity between two texts\"\"\"\n        norm1 = self._normalize_text(text1)\n        norm2 = self._normalize_text(text2)\n\n        matcher = SequenceMatcher(None, norm1, norm2)\n        return matcher.ratio()\n\n    def _token_overlap(self, tokens1: List[str], tokens2: List[str]) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Compute token overlap ratio\"\"\"\n        set1 = set(tokens1)\n        set2 = set(tokens2)\n\n        if not set1 or not set2:\n            return 0.0\n\n        intersection = len(set1 & set2)\n        union = len(set1 | set2)\n\n        return intersection / union if union > 0 else 0.0\n\n    def _contains_keywords(self, span_tokens: List[str], entry: CodebookEntry) -> Tuple[bool, float]:\n        \"\"\"Check if span contains codebook keywords\"\"\"\n        span_text_norm = self._normalize_text(' '.join(span_tokens))\n\n        match_count = 0\n        max_matches = 0\n\n        for keyword in entry.keywords_examples:\n            kw_norm = self._normalize_text(keyword)\n            max_matches += 1\n\n            # Exact substring match (normalized)\n            if kw_norm in span_text_norm:\n                match_count += 1\n            # Token overlap\n            elif kw_norm in [t.lower() for t in span_tokens]:\n                match_count += 1\n\n        if max_matches == 0:\n            return False, 0.0\n\n        return match_count > 0, match_count / max_matches\n\n    def _compute_match_score(self, span_text: str, entry: CodebookEntry,\n                            station: str, fail_code: str, test_item: str) -> Tuple[float, str]:\n        \"\"\"Compute match score for a segment against a codebook entry\"\"\"\n        evidence_scores = []\n        evidence_details = []\n\n        span_tokens = self._tokenize(span_text)\n        span_norm = self._normalize_text(span_text)\n\n        # 1. Station compatibility check\n        if entry.station_scope:\n            station_match = station in entry.station_scope\n            if not station_match:\n                # Station mismatch is a strong negative signal\n                return 0.0, \"station mismatch\"\n\n        # 2. Text similarity with standard label\n        label_sim = self._text_similarity(span_text, entry.standard_label)\n        evidence_scores.append(('label_similarity', label_sim, 0.25))\n        if label_sim > 0.5:\n            evidence_details.append(f\"label_sim:{label_sim:.3f}\")\n\n        # 3. Keyword matching\n        has_keywords, keyword_score = self._contains_keywords(span_tokens, entry)\n        evidence_scores.append(('keyword_match', float(has_keywords), 0.35))\n        if has_keywords:\n            evidence_details.append(f\"keywords:{keyword_score:.3f}\")\n\n        # 4. Token overlap with keywords\n        label_tokens = self._tokenize(entry.standard_label)\n        token_overlap = self._token_overlap(span_tokens, label_tokens)\n        evidence_scores.append(('token_overlap', token_overlap, 0.20))\n        if token_overlap > 0.2:\n            evidence_details.append(f\"token_overlap:{token_overlap:.3f}\")\n\n        # 5. Category relevance (weak signal)\n        category_score = 0.0\n        # Check for common category patterns\n        if entry.category_lv2 in ['OPEN', 'SHORT'] and any(kw in span_norm for kw in ['open', '开路', 'short', '短路']):\n            category_score = 0.3\n        elif entry.category_lv2 in ['FIXTURE_CONTACT'] and any(kw in span_norm for kw in ['fixture', '治具', 'contact', '接触', 'probe', '探针']):\n            category_score = 0.3\n        elif entry.category_lv2 in ['COLD_SOLDER'] and any(kw in span_norm for kw in ['solder', '焊', 'cold', 'float', '浮']):\n            category_score = 0.3\n        elif entry.category_lv2 in ['FLASH_FAIL'] and any(kw in span_norm for kw in ['flash', 'program', '烧录', '写入']):\n            category_score = 0.3\n\n        evidence_scores.append(('category_match', category_score, 0.10))\n        if category_score > 0:\n            evidence_details.append(f\"category_match:{category_score:.3f}\")\n\n        # Compute weighted score\n        total_weight = sum(w for _, _, w in evidence_scores)\n        final_score = sum(s * w for _, s, w in evidence_scores) / total_weight if total_weight > 0 else 0.0\n\n        # Boost score if text has strong keyword presence\n        if has_keywords and keyword_score > 0.5:\n            final_score = min(1.0, final_score * 1.15)\n\n        rationale = ', '.join(evidence_details) if evidence_details else \"weak_match\"\n\n        return final_score, rationale\n\n    def _deterministic_tiebreak(self, candidates: List[Tuple[str, float, str]],\n                               record_id: str, segment_idx: int, station: str,\n                               fail_code: str, test_item: str) -> int:\n        \"\"\"Deterministic tiebreak for near-equivalent candidates\"\"\"\n        # If candidates are very close in score, use seed to pick one\n        if len(candidates) > 1:\n            top_score = candidates[0][1]\n            margin = 0.05  # Small margin for tie-breaking\n\n            tied_indices = [i for i, (_, score, _) in enumerate(candidates)\n                          if abs(score - top_score) <= margin]\n\n            if len(tied_indices) > 1:\n                # Use seed to pick deterministically among tied candidates\n                chosen_idx = tied_indices[seed % len(tied_indices)]\n                return chosen_idx\n\n        return 0  # Top candidate by score\n\n    def normalize_record(self, record: Dict, product_id: str, station: str, engineer_id: str) -> NormalizedRecord:\n        \"\"\"Normalize a single record\"\"\"\n        record_id = record['record_id']\n        raw_reason_text = record.get('raw_reason_text', '').strip()\n        fail_code = record.get('fail_code', '')\n        test_item = record.get('test_item', '')\n\n        normalized_segments = []\n\n        if not raw_reason_text:\n            # Empty reason text\n            return NormalizedRecord(record_id, product_id, station, engineer_id, raw_reason_text, [])\n\n        # Segment the raw text\n        segments = self._segment_text(raw_reason_text)\n\n        # Get codebook for this product\n        if product_id not in self.codebooks:\n            return NormalizedRecord(record_id, product_id, station, engineer_id, raw_reason_text, [])\n\n        codebook_entries = self.codebooks[product_id]\n\n        for seg_idx, span_text in enumerate(segments, 1):\n            segment_id = f\"{record_id}-S{seg_idx}\"\n\n            # Score all candidates\n            candidates = []\n            for entry in codebook_entries:\n                score, rationale = self._compute_match_score(span_text, entry, station, fail_code, test_item)\n                if score > 0:\n                    candidates.append((entry.code, score, rationale))\n\n            # Sort by score descending\n            candidates.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n            if not candidates:\n                # No match found\n                norm_seg = NormalizedSegment(\n                    segment_id=segment_id,\n                    span_text=span_text,\n                    pred_code=\"UNKNOWN\",\n                    pred_label=\"\",\n                    confidence=0.2,\n                    rationale=\"no_match_found\"\n                )\n            else:\n                # Apply tiebreak if needed\n                chosen_idx = self._deterministic_tiebreak(\n                    candidates, record_id, seg_idx, station, fail_code, test_item\n                )\n\n                chosen_code, chosen_score, rationale = candidates[chosen_idx]\n\n                # Confidence calibration\n                if chosen_score < 0.4:\n                    # Low confidence - mark as UNKNOWN\n                    pred_code = \"UNKNOWN\"\n                    pred_label = \"\"\n                    confidence = min(0.35, chosen_score + 0.1)\n                else:\n                    pred_code = chosen_code\n                    # Find the full label\n                    pred_label = next((e.standard_label for e in codebook_entries if e.code == chosen_code), \"\")\n\n                    # Confidence based on score and rank\n                    confidence = min(0.99, chosen_score)\n\n                    # Check if there's a close second candidate\n                    if len(candidates) > 1:\n                        _, second_score, _ = candidates[1]\n                        score_gap = chosen_score - second_score\n                        if score_gap < 0.15:  # Close competition\n                            confidence *= 0.85\n\n            norm_seg = NormalizedSegment(\n                segment_id=segment_id,\n                span_text=span_text,\n                pred_code=pred_code if 'pred_code' in locals() else \"UNKNOWN\",\n                pred_label=pred_label if 'pred_label' in locals() else \"\",\n                confidence=round(confidence, 4),\n                rationale=rationale\n            )\n\n            normalized_segments.append(norm_seg)\n\n        return NormalizedRecord(record_id, product_id, station, engineer_id, raw_reason_text, normalized_segments)\n\n    def process_logs(self, log_path: str, output_path: str):\n        \"\"\"Process all records from test center logs\"\"\"\n        records = []\n\n        with open(log_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n            for row_idx, row in enumerate(reader):\n                if row_idx % 1000 == 0:\n                    print(f\"Processing record {row_idx + 1}...\")\n\n                record_id = row['record_id']\n                product_id = row.get('product_id', '').strip()\n                station = row.get('station', '').strip()\n                engineer_id = row.get('engineer_id', '').strip()\n\n                # Build station scope map\n            for entry in entries:\n                key = (product_id, entry.code)\n                self.station_scope_map[key] = entry.station_scope\n\n    def _normalize_text(self, text: str) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Normalize text for comparison\"\"\"\n        # Convert to lowercase, remove extra spaces\n        text = text.lower().strip()\n        # Remove punctuation and normalize whitespace\n        text = re.sub(r'[\\s\\-_/,;\\.]+', ' ', text)\n        return text\n\n    def _tokenize(self, text: str) -> List[str]:\n        \"\"\"Tokenize text into meaningful units\"\"\"\n        text = self._normalize_text(text)\n        # Split on whitespace and punctuation\n        tokens = re.findall(r'\\b\\w+\\b', text)\n        return tokens\n\n    def _segment_text(self, raw_text: str) -> List[str]:\n        \"\"\"Split raw_reason_text into logical segments\"\"\"\n        if not raw_text or not raw_text.strip():\n            return []\n\n        # Split on common separators: comma, slash, Chinese full-width punctuation\n        # Patterns: comma, slash, \"and\" (中文\"和\"), semicolon, Chinese period\n        segments = re.split(r'[,/;，；、]|(?<=。)', raw_text)\n\n        # Clean segments: strip whitespace and filter out empty\n        segments = [s.strip() for s in segments if s.strip()]\n\n        # If only one segment, it might contain multiple issues\n        if len(segments) == 1 and len(raw_text) > 50:\n            # Try splitting by English keywords\n            text = raw_text\n            # Look for patterns like \"A, B / C\" or \"A及B\"\n            if ' / ' in text or ' and ' in text.lower():\n                parts = re.split(r'\\s+/\\s+|\\s+and\\s+', text, flags=re.IGNORECASE)\n                if len(parts) > 1:\n                    segments = [p.strip() for p in parts if p.strip()]\n            # Chinese \"及\"\n            elif '及' in text:\n                parts = text.split('及')\n                if len(parts) > 1:\n                    segments = [p.strip() for p in parts if p.strip()]\n\n        return segments if segments else [raw_text]\n\n    def _text_similarity(self, text1: str, text2: str) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Compute similarity between two texts\"\"\"\n        norm1 = self._normalize_text(text1)\n        norm2 = self._normalize_text(text2)\n\n        matcher = SequenceMatcher(None, norm1, norm2)\n        return matcher.ratio()\n\n    def _token_overlap(self, tokens1: List[str], tokens2: List[str]) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Compute token overlap ratio\"\"\"\n        set1 = set(tokens1)\n        set2 = set(tokens2)\n\n        if not set1 or not set2:\n            return 0.0\n\n        intersection = len(set1 & set2)\n        union = len(set1 | set2)\n\n        return intersection / union if union > 0 else 0.0\n\n    def _contains_keywords(self, span_tokens: List[str], entry: CodebookEntry) -> Tuple[bool, float]:\n        \"\"\"Check if span contains codebook keywords\"\"\"\n        span_text_norm = self._normalize_text(' '.join(span_tokens))\n\n        match_count = 0\n        max_matches = 0\n\n        for keyword in entry.keywords_examples:\n            kw_norm = self._normalize_text(keyword)\n            max_matches += 1\n\n            # Exact substring match (normalized)\n            if kw_norm in span_text_norm:\n                match_count += 1\n            # Token overlap\n            elif kw_norm in [t.lower() for t in span_tokens]:\n                match_count += 1\n\n        if max_matches == 0:\n            return False, 0.0\n\n        return match_count > 0, match_count / max_matches\n\n    def _compute_match_score(self, span_text: str, entry: CodebookEntry,\n                            station: str, fail_code: str, test_item: str) -> Tuple[float, str]:\n        \"\"\"Compute match score for a segment against a codebook entry\"\"\"\n        evidence_scores = []\n        evidence_details = []\n\n        span_tokens = self._tokenize(span_text)\n        span_norm = self._normalize_text(span_text)\n\n        # 1. Station compatibility check\n        if entry.station_scope:\n            station_match = station in entry.station_scope\n            if not station_match:\n                # Station mismatch is a strong negative signal\n                return 0.0, \"station mismatch\"\n\n        # 2. Text similarity with standard label\n        label_sim = self._text_similarity(span_text, entry.standard_label)\n        evidence_scores.append(('label_similarity', label_sim, 0.25))\n        if label_sim > 0.5:\n            evidence_details.append(f\"label_sim:{label_sim:.3f}\")\n\n        # 3. Keyword matching\n        has_keywords, keyword_score = self._contains_keywords(span_tokens, entry)\n        evidence_scores.append(('keyword_match', float(has_keywords), 0.35))\n        if has_keywords:\n            evidence_details.append(f\"keywords:{keyword_score:.3f}\")\n\n        # 4. Token overlap with keywords\n        label_tokens = self._tokenize(entry.standard_label)\n        token_overlap = self._token_overlap(span_tokens, label_tokens)\n        evidence_scores.append(('token_overlap', token_overlap, 0.20))\n        if token_overlap > 0.2:\n            evidence_details.append(f\"token_overlap:{token_overlap:.3f}\")\n\n        # 5. Category relevance (weak signal)\n        category_score = 0.0\n        # Check for common category patterns\n        if entry.category_lv2 in ['OPEN', 'SHORT'] and any(kw in span_norm for kw in ['open', '开路', 'short', '短路']):\n            category_score = 0.3\n        elif entry.category_lv2 in ['FIXTURE_CONTACT'] and any(kw in span_norm for kw in ['fixture', '治具', 'contact', '接触', 'probe', '探针']):\n            category_score = 0.3\n        elif entry.category_lv2 in ['COLD_SOLDER'] and any(kw in span_norm for kw in ['solder', '焊', 'cold', 'float', '浮']):\n            category_score = 0.3\n        elif entry.category_lv2 in ['FLASH_FAIL'] and any(kw in span_norm for kw in ['flash', 'program', '烧录', '写入']):\n            category_score = 0.3\n\n        evidence_scores.append(('category_match', category_score, 0.10))\n        if category_score > 0:\n            evidence_details.append(f\"category_match:{category_score:.3f}\")\n\n        # Compute weighted score\n        total_weight = sum(w for _, _, w in evidence_scores)\n        final_score = sum(s * w for _, s, w in evidence_scores) / total_weight if total_weight > 0 else 0.0\n\n        # Boost score if text has strong keyword presence\n        if has_keywords and keyword_score > 0.5:\n            final_score = min(1.0, final_score * 1.15)\n\n        rationale = ', '.join(evidence_details) if evidence_details else \"weak_match\"\n\n        return final_score, rationale\n\n    def _deterministic_tiebreak(self, candidates: List[Tuple[str, float, str]],\n                               record_id: str, segment_idx: int, station: str,\n                               fail_code: str, test_item: str) -> int:\n        \"\"\"Deterministic tiebreak for near-equivalent candidates\"\"\"\n        # Create a hash seed from context\n        context = f\"{record_id}-{segment_idx}-{station}-{fail_code}-{test_item}\"\n        seed = int(hashlib.md5(context.encode()).hexdigest(), 16) % 100\n\n        # If candidates are very close in score, use seed to pick one\n        if len(candidates) > 1:\n            top_score = candidates[0][1]\n            margin = 0.05  # Small margin for tie-breaking\n\n            tied_indices = [i for i, (_, score, _) in enumerate(candidates)\n                          if abs(score - top_score) <= margin]\n\n            if len(tied_indices) > 1:\n                # Use seed to pick deterministically among tied candidates\n                chosen_idx = tied_indices[seed % len(tied_indices)]\n                return chosen_idx\n\n        return 0  # Top candidate by score\n\n    def normalize_record(self, record: Dict, product_id: str, station: str, engineer_id: str) -> NormalizedRecord:\n        \"\"\"Normalize a single record\"\"\"\n        record_id = record['record_id']\n        raw_reason_text = record.get('raw_reason_text', '').strip()\n        fail_code = record.get('fail_code', '')\n        test_item = record.get('test_item', '')\n\n        normalized_segments = []\n\n        if not raw_reason_text:\n            # Empty reason text\n            return NormalizedRecord(record_id, product_id, station, engineer_id, raw_reason_text, [])\n\n        # Segment the raw text\n        segments = self._segment_text(raw_reason_text)\n\n        # Get codebook for this product\n        if product_id not in self.codebooks:\n            return NormalizedRecord(record_id, product_id, station, engineer_id, raw_reason_text, [])\n\n        codebook_entries = self.codebooks[product_id]\n\n        for seg_idx, span_text in enumerate(segments, 1):\n            segment_id = f\"{record_id}-S{seg_idx}\"\n\n            # Score all candidates\n            candidates = []\n            for entry in codebook_entries:\n                score, rationale = self._compute_match_score(span_text, entry, station, fail_code, test_item)\n                if score > 0:\n                    candidates.append((entry.code, score, rationale))\n\n            # Sort by score descending\n            candidates.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n            if not candidates:\n                # No match found\n                norm_seg = NormalizedSegment(\n                    segment_id=segment_id,\n                    span_text=span_text,\n                    pred_code=\"UNKNOWN\",\n                    pred_label=\"\",\n                    confidence=0.2,\n                    rationale=\"no_match_found\"\n                )\n            else:\n                # Apply tiebreak if needed\n                chosen_idx = self._deterministic_tiebreak(\n                    candidates, record_id, seg_idx, station, fail_code, test_item\n                )\n\n                chosen_code, chosen_score, rationale = candidates[chosen_idx]\n\n                # Confidence calibration\n                if chosen_score < 0.4:\n                    # Low confidence - mark as UNKNOWN\n                    pred_code = \"UNKNOWN\"\n                    pred_label = \"\"\n                    confidence = min(0.35, chosen_score + 0.1)\n                else:\n                    pred_code = chosen_code\n                    # Find the full label\n                    pred_label = next((e.standard_label for e in codebook_entries if e.code == chosen_code), \"\")\n\n                    # Confidence based on score and rank\n                    confidence = min(0.99, chosen_score)\n\n                    # Check if there's a close second candidate\n                    if len(candidates) > 1:\n                        _, second_score, _ = candidates[1]\n                        score_gap = chosen_score - second_score\n                        if score_gap < 0.15:  # Close competition\n                            confidence *= 0.85\n\n            norm_seg = NormalizedSegment(\n                segment_id=segment_id,\n                span_text=span_text,\n                pred_code=pred_code if 'pred_code' in locals() else \"UNKNOWN\",\n                pred_label=pred_label if 'pred_label' in locals() else \"\",\n                confidence=round(confidence, 4),\n                rationale=rationale\n            )\n\n            normalized_segments.append(norm_seg)\n\n        return NormalizedRecord(record_id, product_id, station, engineer_id, raw_reason_text, normalized_segments)\n\n    def process_logs(self, log_path: str, output_path: str):\n        \"\"\"Process all records from test center logs\"\"\"\n        records = []\n\n        with open(log_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n            for row_idx, row in enumerate(reader):\n                if row_idx % 1000 == 0:\n                    print(f\"Processing record {row_idx + 1}...\")\n\n                record_id = row['record_id']\n                product_id = row.get('product_id', '').strip()\n                station = row.get('station', '').strip()\n                engineer_id = row.get('engineer_id', '').strip()\n\n                # Validate product\n                if product_id not in self.codebooks:\n                    print(f\"Warning: Unknown product {product_id} for record {record_id}\")\n                    continue\n\n                normalized = self.normalize_record(row, product_id, station, engineer_id)\n                records.append(normalized)\n\n        # Write output\n        output_data = {\n            \"records\": [asdict(r) for r in records]\n        }\n\n        # Convert NormalizedSegment dataclass dicts to proper format\n        for record in output_data[\"records\"]:\n            record[\"normalized\"] = [asdict(seg) for seg in records[output_data[\"records\"].index(record)].normalized]\n\n        with open(output_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            json.dump(output_data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\n\n        print(f\"Processed {len(records)} records\")\n        print(f\"Output written to {output_path}\")\n\n        # Print statistics\n        self._print_statistics(records)\n\n    def _print_statistics(self, records: List[NormalizedRecord]):\n        \"\"\"Print statistics about the normalization\"\"\"\n        total_segments = sum(len(r.normalized) for r in records)\n        unknown_segments = sum(1 for r in records for seg in r.normalized if seg.pred_code == \"UNKNOWN\")\n\n        confidences = [seg.confidence for r in records for seg in r.normalized]\n        unknown_confidences = [seg.confidence for r in records for seg in r.normalized if seg.pred_code == \"UNKNOWN\"]\n        known_confidences = [seg.confidence for r in records for seg in r.normalized if seg.pred_code != \"UNKNOWN\"]\n\n        if confidences:\n            avg_conf = sum(confidences) / len(confidences)\n            print(f\"\\nStatistics:\")\n            print(f\"  Total records: {len(records)}\")\n            print(f\"  Total segments: {total_segments}\")\n            print(f\"  UNKNOWN predictions: {unknown_segments} ({100*unknown_segments/total_segments:.1f}%)\")\n            print(f\"  Average confidence: {avg_conf:.4f}\")\n            if unknown_confidences:\n                print(f\"  Average UNKNOWN confidence: {sum(unknown_confidences)/len(unknown_confidences):.4f}\")\n            if known_confidences:\n                print(f\"  Average known confidence: {sum(known_confidences)/len(known_confidences):.4f}\")\n\ndef main():\n    normalizer = CodebookNormalizer()\n\n    # product_id -> list of CodebookEntry\n        self.valid_code_set = {}  # product_id -> set of codes\n        self.station_scope_map = {}  # (product_id, code) -> set of stations\n        self.keyword_index = {}  # product_id -> code -> normalized keywords\n\n    def load_codebooks(self, codebook_paths: Dict[str, str]):\n        \"\"\"Load all product codebooks\"\"\"\n        for product_id, path in codebook_paths.items():\n            entries = []\n            with open(path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n                reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n                for row in reader:\n                    station_scope = set(s.strip() for s in row['station_scope'].split(';'))\n                    keywords = set(s.strip() for s in row['keywords_examples'].split(','))\n\n                    entry = CodebookEntry(\n                        product_id=row['product_id'],\n                        code=row['code'],\n                        standard_label=row['standard_label'],\n                        category_lv1=row['category_lv1'],\n                        category_lv2=row['category_lv2'],\n                        station_scope=station_scope,\n                        keywords_examples=keywords\n                    )\n                    entries.append(entry)\n\n            self.codebooks[product_id] = entries\n            self.valid_code_set[product_id] = {e.code for e in entries}\n\n            # Build keyword index\n            self.keyword_index[product_id] = {}\n            for entry in entries:\n                normalized_keywords = set()\n                for kw in entry.keywords_examples:\n                    normalized_keywords.add(self._normalize_text(kw))\n                self.keyword_index[product_id][entry.code] = normalized_keywords\n\n            # Build station scope map\n            for entry in entries:\n                key = (product_id, entry.code)\n                self.station_scope_map[key] = entry.station_scope\n\n    def _normalize_text(self, text: str) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Normalize text for comparison\"\"\"\n        # Convert to lowercase, remove extra spaces\n        text = text.lower().strip()\n        # Remove punctuation and normalize whitespace\n        text = re.sub(r'[\\s\\-_/,;\\.]+', ' ', text)\n        return text\n\n    def _tokenize(self, text: str) -> List[str]:\n        \"\"\"Tokenize text into meaningful units\"\"\"\n        text = self._normalize_text(text)\n        # Split on whitespace and punctuation\n        tokens = re.findall(r'\\b\\w+\\b', text)\n        return tokens\n\n    def _segment_text(self, raw_text: str) -> List[str]:\n        \"\"\"Split raw_reason_text into logical segments\"\"\"\n        if not raw_text or not raw_text.strip():\n            return []\n\n        # Split on common separators: comma, slash, Chinese full-width punctuation\n        # Patterns: comma, slash, \"and\" (中文\"和\"), semicolon, Chinese period\n        segments = re.split(r'[,/;，；、]|(?<=。)', raw_text)\n\n        # Clean segments: strip whitespace and filter out empty\n        segments = [s.strip() for s in segments if s.strip()]\n\n        # If only one segment, it might contain multiple issues\n        if len(segments) == 1 and len(raw_text) > 50:\n            # Try splitting by English keywords\n            text = raw_text\n            # Look for patterns like \"A, B / C\" or \"A及B\"\n            if ' / ' in text or ' and ' in text.lower():\n                parts = re.split(r'\\s+/\\s+|\\s+and\\s+', text, flags=re.IGNORECASE)\n                if len(parts) > 1:\n                    segments = [p.strip() for p in parts if p.strip()]\n            # Chinese \"及\"\n            elif '及' in text:\n                parts = text.split('及')\n                if len(parts) > 1:\n                    segments = [p.strip() for p in parts if p.strip()]\n\n        return segments if segments else [raw_text]\n\n    def _text_similarity(self, text1: str, text2: str) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Compute similarity between two texts\"\"\"\n        norm1 = self._normalize_text(text1)\n        norm2 = self._normalize_text(text2)\n\n        matcher = SequenceMatcher(None, norm1, norm2)\n        return matcher.ratio()\n\n    def _token_overlap(self, tokens1: List[str], tokens2: List[str]) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Compute token overlap ratio\"\"\"\n        set1 = set(tokens1)\n        set2 = set(tokens2)\n\n        if not set1 or not set2:\n            return 0.0\n\n        intersection = len(set1 & set2)\n        union = len(set1 | set2)\n\n        return intersection / union if union > 0 else 0.0\n\n    def _contains_keywords(self, span_tokens: List[str], entry: CodebookEntry) -> Tuple[bool, float]:\n        \"\"\"Check if span contains codebook keywords\"\"\"\n        span_text_norm = self._normalize_text(' '.join(span_tokens))\n\n        match_count = 0\n        max_matches = 0\n\n        for keyword in entry.keywords_examples:\n            kw_norm = self._normalize_text(keyword)\n            max_matches += 1\n\n            # Exact substring match (normalized)\n            if kw_norm in span_text_norm:\n                match_count += 1\n            # Token overlap\n            elif kw_norm in [t.lower() for t in span_tokens]:\n                match_count += 1\n\n        if max_matches == 0:\n            return False, 0.0\n\n        return match_count > 0, match_count / max_matches\n\n    def _compute_match_score(self, span_text: str, entry: CodebookEntry,\n                            station: str, fail_code: str, test_item: str) -> Tuple[float, str]:\n        \"\"\"Compute match score for a segment against a codebook entry\"\"\"\n        evidence_scores = []\n        evidence_details = []\n\n        span_tokens = self._tokenize(span_text)\n        span_norm = self._normalize_text(span_text)\n\n        # 1. Station compatibility check\n        if entry.station_scope:\n            station_match = station in entry.station_scope\n            if not station_match:\n                # Station mismatch is a strong negative signal\n                return 0.0, \"station mismatch\"\n\n        # 2. Text similarity with standard label\n        label_sim = self._text_similarity(span_text, entry.standard_label)\n        evidence_scores.append(('label_similarity', label_sim, 0.25))\n        if label_sim > 0.5:\n            evidence_details.append(f\"label_sim:{label_sim:.3f}\")\n\n        # 3. Keyword matching\n        has_keywords, keyword_score = self._contains_keywords(span_tokens, entry)\n        evidence_scores.append(('keyword_match', float(has_keywords), 0.35))\n        if has_keywords:\n            evidence_details.append(f\"keywords:{keyword_score:.3f}\")\n\n        # 4. Token overlap with keywords\n        label_tokens = self._tokenize(entry.standard_label)\n        token_overlap = self._token_overlap(span_tokens, label_tokens)\n        evidence_scores.append(('token_overlap', token_overlap, 0.20))\n        if token_overlap > 0.2:\n            evidence_details.append(f\"token_overlap:{token_overlap:.3f}\")\n\n        # 5. Category relevance (weak signal)\n        category_score = 0.0\n        # Check for common category patterns\n        if entry.category_lv2 in ['OPEN', 'SHORT'] and any(kw in span_norm for kw in ['open', '开路', 'short', '短路']):\n            category_score = 0.3\n        elif entry.category_lv2 in ['FIXTURE_CONTACT'] and any(kw in span_norm for kw in ['fixture', '治具', 'contact', '接触', 'probe', '探针']):\n            category_score = 0.3\n        elif entry.category_lv2 in ['COLD_SOLDER'] and any(kw in span_norm for kw in ['solder', '焊', 'cold', 'float', '浮']):\n            category_score = 0.3\n        elif entry.category_lv2 in ['FLASH_FAIL'] and any(kw in span_norm for kw in ['flash', 'program', '烧录', '写入']):\n            category_score = 0.3\n\n        evidence_scores.append(('category_match', category_score, 0.10))\n        if category_score > 0:\n            evidence_details.append(f\"category_match:{category_score:.3f}\")\n\n        # Compute weighted score\n        total_weight = sum(w for _, _, w in evidence_scores)\n        final_score = sum(s * w for _, s, w in evidence_scores) / total_weight if total_weight > 0 else 0.0\n\n        # Boost score if text has strong keyword presence\n        if has_keywords and keyword_score > 0.5:\n            final_score = min(1.0, final_score * 1.15)\n\n        rationale = ', '.join(evidence_details) if evidence_details else \"weak_match\"\n\n        return final_score, rationale\n\n    def _deterministic_tiebreak(self, candidates: List[Tuple[str, float, str]],\n                               record_id: str, segment_idx: int, station: str,\n                               fail_code: str, test_item: str) -> int:\n        \"\"\"Deterministic tiebreak for near-equivalent candidates\"\"\"\n        # Create a hash seed from context\n        context = f\"{record_id}-{segment_idx}-{station}-{fail_code}-{test_item}\"\n        seed = int(hashlib.md5(context.encode()).hexdigest(), 16) % 100\n\n        # If candidates are very close in score, use seed to pick one\n        if len(candidates) > 1:\n            top_score = candidates[0][1]\n            margin = 0.05  # Small margin for tie-breaking\n\n            tied_indices = [i for i, (_, score, _) in enumerate(candidates)\n                          if abs(score - top_score) <= margin]\n\n            if len(tied_indices) > 1:\n                # Use seed to pick deterministically among tied candidates\n                chosen_idx = tied_indices[seed % len(tied_indices)]\n                return chosen_idx\n\n        return 0  # Top candidate by score\n\n    def normalize_record(self, record: Dict, product_id: str, station: str, engineer_id: str) -> NormalizedRecord:\n        \"\"\"Normalize a single record\"\"\"\n        record_id = record['record_id']\n        raw_reason_text = record.get('raw_reason_text', '').strip()\n        fail_code = record.get('fail_code', '')\n        test_item = record.get('test_item', '')\n\n        normalized_segments = []\n\n        if not raw_reason_text:\n            # Empty reason text\n            return NormalizedRecord(record_id, product_id, station, engineer_id, raw_reason_text, [])\n\n        # Segment the raw text\n        segments = self._segment_text(raw_reason_text)\n\n        # Get codebook for this product\n        if product_id not in self.codebooks:\n            return NormalizedRecord(record_id, product_id, station, engineer_id, raw_reason_text, [])\n\n        codebook_entries = self.codebooks[product_id]\n\n        for seg_idx, span_text in enumerate(segments, 1):\n            segment_id = f\"{record_id}-S{seg_idx}\"\n\n            # Score all candidates\n            candidates = []\n            for entry in codebook_entries:\n                score, rationale = self._compute_match_score(span_text, entry, station, fail_code, test_item)\n                if score > 0:\n                    candidates.append((entry.code, score, rationale))\n\n            # Sort by score descending\n            candidates.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n            if not candidates:\n                # No match found\n                norm_seg = NormalizedSegment(\n                    segment_id=segment_id,\n                    span_text=span_text,\n                    pred_code=\"UNKNOWN\",\n                    pred_label=\"\",\n                    confidence=0.2,\n                    rationale=\"no_match_found\"\n                )\n            else:\n                # Apply tiebreak if needed\n                chosen_idx = self._deterministic_tiebreak(\n                    candidates, record_id, seg_idx, station, fail_code, test_item\n                )\n\n                chosen_code, chosen_score, rationale = candidates[chosen_idx]\n\n                # Confidence calibration\n                if chosen_score < 0.4:\n                    # Low confidence - mark as UNKNOWN\n                    pred_code = \"UNKNOWN\"\n                    pred_label = \"\"\n                    confidence = min(0.35, chosen_score + 0.1)\n                else:\n                    pred_code = chosen_code\n                    # Find the full label\n                    pred_label = next((e.standard_label for e in codebook_entries if e.code == chosen_code), \"\")\n\n                    # Confidence based on score and rank\n                    confidence = min(0.99, chosen_score)\n\n                    # Check if there's a close second candidate\n                    if len(candidates) > 1:\n                        _, second_score, _ = candidates[1]\n                        score_gap = chosen_score - second_score\n                        if score_gap < 0.15:  # Close competition\n                            confidence *= 0.85\n\n            norm_seg = NormalizedSegment(\n                segment_id=segment_id,\n                span_text=span_text,\n                pred_code=pred_code if 'pred_code' in locals() else \"UNKNOWN\",\n                pred_label=pred_label if 'pred_label' in locals() else \"\",\n                confidence=round(confidence, 4),\n                rationale=rationale\n            )\n\n            normalized_segments.append(norm_seg)\n\n        return NormalizedRecord(record_id, product_id, station, engineer_id, raw_reason_text, normalized_segments)\n\n    def process_logs(self, log_path: str, output_path: str):\n        \"\"\"Process all records from test center logs\"\"\"\n        records = []\n\n        with open(log_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n            for row_idx, row in enumerate(reader):\n                if row_idx % 1000 == 0:\n                    print(f\"Processing record {row_idx + 1}...\")\n\n                record_id = row['record_id']\n                product_id = row.get('product_id', '').strip()\n                station = row.get('station', '').strip()\n                engineer_id = row.get('engineer_id', '').strip()\n\n                # Validate product\n                if product_id not in self.codebooks:\n                    print(f\"Warning: Unknown product {product_id} for record {record_id}\")\n                    continue\n\n                normalized = self.normalize_record(row, product_id, station, engineer_id)\n                records.append(normalized)\n\n        # Write output\n        output_data = {\n            \"records\": [asdict(r) for r in records]\n        }\n\n        # Convert NormalizedSegment dataclass dicts to proper format\n        for record in output_data[\"records\"]:\n            record[\"normalized\"] = [asdict(seg) for seg in records[output_data[\"records\"].index(record)].normalized]\n\n        with open(output_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            json.dump(output_data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\n\n        print(f\"Processed {len(records)} records\")\n        print(f\"Output written to {output_path}\")\n\n        # Print statistics\n        self._print_statistics(records)\n\n    def _print_statistics(self, records: List[NormalizedRecord]):\n        \"\"\"Print statistics about the normalization\"\"\"\n        total_segments = sum(len(r.normalized) for r in records)\n        unknown_segments = sum(1 for r in records for seg in r.normalized if seg.pred_code == \"UNKNOWN\")\n\n        confidences = [seg.confidence for r in records for seg in r.normalized]\n        unknown_confidences = [seg.confidence for r in records for seg in r.normalized if seg.pred_code == \"UNKNOWN\"]\n        known_confidences = [seg.confidence for r in records for seg in r.normalized if seg.pred_code != \"UNKNOWN\"]\n\n        if confidences:\n            avg_conf = sum(confidences) / len(confidences)\n            print(f\"\\nStatistics:\")\n            print(f\"  Total records: {len(records)}\")\n            print(f\"  Total segments: {total_segments}\")\n            print(f\"  UNKNOWN predictions: {unknown_segments} ({100*unknown_segments/total_segments:.1f}%)\")\n            print(f\"  Average confidence: {avg_conf:.4f}\")\n            if unknown_confidences:\n                print(f\"  Average UNKNOWN confidence: {sum(unknown_confidences)/len(unknown_confidences):.4f}\")\n            if known_confidences:\n                print(f\"  Average known confidence: {sum(known_confidences)/len(known_confidences):.4f}\")\n\ndef main():\n    normalizer = CodebookNormalizer()\n\n    # python3\n\"\"\"\nManufacturing Failure Reason Codebook Normalization System - v2\n\nImproved version with better segmentation, matching, and confidence calibration.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport csv\nimport json\nimport re\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom typing import List, Dict, Set, Tuple, Optional\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nimport hashlib\n\n@dataclass\nclass CodebookEntry:\n    \"\"\"Represents a single codebook entry\"\"\"\n    product_id: str\n    code: str\n    standard_label: str\n    category_lv1: str\n    category_lv2: str\n    station_scope: Set[str]\n    keywords_examples: List[str]\n\n@dataclass\nclass NormalizedSegment:\n    \"\"\"Represents a normalized segment\"\"\"\n    segment_id: str\n    span_text: str\n    pred_code: str\n    pred_label: str\n    confidence: float\n    rationale: str\n\nclass CodebookNormalizer:\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.codebooks = {}\n        self.valid_code_set = {}\n        self.station_scope_map = {}\n\n    def load_codebooks(self, codebook_paths: Dict[str, str]):\n        \"\"\"Load all product codebooks\"\"\"\n        for product_id, path in codebook_paths.items():\n            entries = []\n            with open(path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n                reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n                for row in reader:\n                    station_scope = set(s.strip() for s in row['station_scope'].split(';'))\n                    keywords = [s.strip() for s in row['keywords_examples'].split(',')]\n\n                    entry = CodebookEntry(\n                        product_id=row['product_id'],\n                        code=row['code'],\n                        standard_label=row['standard_label'],\n                        category_lv1=row['category_lv1'],\n                        category_lv2=row['category_lv2'],\n                        station_scope=station_scope,\n                        keywords_examples=keywords\n                    )\n                    entries.append(entry)\n\n            self.codebooks[product_id] = entries\n            self.valid_code_set[product_id] = {e.code for e in entries}\n\n    def _normalize_text(self, text: str) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Normalize text for comparison\"\"\"\n        text = text.lower().strip()\n        text = re.sub(r'[\\s\\-_/,;．。，；、]+', ' ', text)\n        return text\n\n    def _tokenize(self, text: str) -> List[str]:\n        \"\"\"Tokenize text\"\"\"\n        text = self._normalize_text(text)\n        tokens = re.findall(r'\\b\\w+\\b|[\\u4e00-\\u9fff]+', text)\n        return tokens\n\n    def _smart_segment(self, raw_text: str) -> List[str]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Intelligently segment raw_reason_text into semantic units.\n\n        Rules:\n        - Don't split too aggressively\n        - Keep related phrases together\n        - Split on natural boundaries (punctuation, keywords)\n        \"\"\"\n        if not raw_text or not raw_text.strip():\n            return []\n\n        text = raw_text.strip()\n\n        # Strategy: split on major delimiters but intelligently\n        # Split on: comma, Chinese full-width comma, semicolon, slash when appropriate\n\n        # First, try to identify if this looks like multiple issues described separately\n        # Pattern: \"Issue1, Issue2 / Issue3\"\n\n        # Split on primary separators\n        parts = re.split(r'[,，;；]', text)\n\n        segments = []\n        for part in parts:\n            part = part.strip()\n            if not part:\n                continue\n\n            # For long parts, try to split on \" / \" or \" and \"\n            # But only if they represent truly separate issues\n            if len(part) > 40:\n                # Check for separator patterns that indicate separate issues\n                sub_parts = re.split(r'\\s+/\\s+|\\s+and\\s+', part, flags=re.IGNORECASE)\n                if len(sub_parts) > 1:\n                    segments.extend([p.strip() for p in sub_parts if p.strip()])\n                else:\n                    segments.append(part)\n            else:\n                segments.append(part)\n\n        return segments if segments else [text]\n\n    def _match_to_codebook(self, span_text: str, entry: CodebookEntry,\n                          station: str) -> Tuple[float, str]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Score how well a span matches a codebook entry.\n\n        Returns: (score, evidence_string)\n        \"\"\"\n        if entry.station_scope and station not in entry.station_scope:\n            return 0.0, \"station_mismatch\"\n\n        span_norm = self._normalize_text(span_text)\n        span_tokens = set(self._tokenize(span_text))\n\n        scores = {}\n\n        # 1. Keyword matching (highest priority)\n        keyword_hits = 0\n        for keyword in entry.keywords_examples:\n            kw_norm = self._normalize_text(keyword)\n            kw_tokens = set(self._tokenize(keyword))\n\n            # Check various matching strategies\n            if kw_norm in span_norm or kw_norm == span_norm:\n                keyword_hits += len(kw_tokens)  # Weight by importance\n            else:\n                # Token overlap\n                overlap = len(span_tokens & kw_tokens)\n                if overlap > 0:\n                    keyword_hits += overlap\n\n        if keyword_hits > 0:\n            scores['keyword'] = min(1.0, keyword_hits / (len(span_tokens) + 3))\n        else:\n            scores['keyword'] = 0.0\n\n        # 2. Label similarity\n        label_norm = self._normalize_text(entry.standard_label)\n        label_tokens = set(self._tokenize(entry.standard_label))\n        label_overlap = len(span_tokens & label_tokens) / max(len(span_tokens), len(label_tokens)) if span_tokens and label_tokens else 0\n        scores['label'] = label_overlap\n\n        # 3. Category-based hints\n        category_score = 0.0\n        category = entry.category_lv2\n\n        if category in ['OPEN', 'SHORT']:\n            if any(k in span_norm for k in ['open', '开路']):\n                if category == 'OPEN':\n                    category_score = 0.4\n            elif any(k in span_norm for k in ['short', '短路']):\n                if category == 'SHORT':\n                    category_score = 0.4\n\n        elif category == 'FIXTURE_CONTACT':\n            if any(k in span_norm for k in ['fixture', '治具', 'contact', '接触', 'probe', '探针', 'pogo', 'reseat', '重插']):\n                category_score = 0.5\n\n        elif category == 'COLD_SOLDER':\n            if any(k in span_norm for k in ['solder', '焊', 'cold', '虚', 'float', '浮']):\n                category_score = 0.4\n\n        elif category == 'FLASH_FAIL':\n            if any(k in span_norm for k in ['flash', 'program', '烧录', '写入', 'write']):\n                category_score = 0.4\n\n        scores['category'] = category_score\n\n        # 4. Component reference\n        component_score = 0.0\n        # Check if span mentions any specific component from the label\n        if any(comp in span_norm for comp in ['U', 'R', 'C', 'L', 'Q', 'D', 'T', 'J', 'TP', 'SH']):\n            # Component mentioned - gives credibility\n            if entry.standard_label and any(comp_ref in span_text for comp_ref in entry.standard_label.split('/')):\n                component_score = 0.3\n\n        scores['component'] = component_score\n\n        # Weighted aggregate\n        weights = {\n            'keyword': 0.4,\n            'label': 0.2,\n            'category': 0.25,\n            'component': 0.15\n        }\n\n        final_score = sum(scores.get(k, 0) * w for k, w in weights.items())\n\n        # {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_vrtx_013gBCR25pTUh2xNRN8ind6x","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01Ls9bYUSML3SVfaMd2MpEtu","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/app/normalize_v3.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nManufacturing Failure Reason Codebook Normalization System - v3\n\nPractical version with robust matching and proper confidence calibration.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport csv\nimport json\nimport re\nfrom typing import List, Dict, Set, Tuple\nimport hashlib\n\nclass CodebookNormalizer:\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.codebooks = {}  # product_id -> list of entries\n        self.stations_by_code = {}  # (product, code) -> set of stations\n\n    def load_codebooks(self, codebook_paths: Dict[str, str]):\n        \"\"\"Load all product codebooks\"\"\"\n        for product_id, path in codebook_paths.items():\n            entries = []\n            with open(path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n                reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n                for row in reader:\n                    stations = set(s.strip() for s in row['station_scope'].split(';'))\n                    keywords = [s.strip() for s in row['keywords_examples'].split(',')]\n\n                    entries.append({\n                        'product_id': row['product_id'],\n                        'code': row['code'],\n                        'label': row['standard_label'],\n                        'cat1': row['category_lv1'],\n                        'cat2': row['category_lv2'],\n                        'stations': stations,\n                        'keywords': keywords\n                    })\n\n            self.codebooks[product_id] = entries\n\n    def _normalize(self, text: str) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Normalize for comparison\"\"\"\n        return text.lower().strip()\n\n    def _segment(self, raw_text: str) -> List[str]:\n        \"\"\"Smart segmentation\"\"\"\n        if not raw_text or not raw_text.strip():\n            return []\n\n        text = raw_text.strip()\n\n        # Split on major punctuation\n        parts = re.split(r'[,，;；]', text)\n\n        segments = []\n        for part in parts:\n            part = part.strip()\n            if not part:\n                continue\n\n            # For longer parts, try \"/\" split but be conservative\n            if len(part) > 50 and ' / ' in part:\n                sub = re.split(r'\\s+/\\s+', part)\n                if len(sub) <= 3:  # Only split if reasonable\n                    segments.extend([p.strip() for p in sub if p.strip()])\n                else:\n                    segments.append(part)\n            else:\n                segments.append(part)\n\n        return segments if segments else [text]\n\n    def _score_match(self, span: str, entry: Dict, station: str) -> Tuple[float, str]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Score how well span matches entry.\n        Returns: (score 0-1, evidence_string)\n        \"\"\"\n        # Station check\n        if entry['stations'] and station not in entry['stations']:\n            return 0.0, \"station_mismatch\"\n\n        span_norm = self._normalize(span)\n        span_lower = span.lower()\n\n        # Count keyword matches\n        keyword_matches = 0\n        total_keywords = len(entry['keywords'])\n\n        for kw in entry['keywords']:\n            kw_norm = self._normalize(kw)\n            # Check if keyword is in span (normalized or original)\n            if kw_norm in span_norm or kw in span or kw.lower() in span_lower:\n                keyword_matches += 1\n\n        if total_keywords == 0:\n            keyword_score = 0.0\n        else:\n            keyword_score = keyword_matches / total_keywords\n\n        # Category matching (bonus)\n        cat2 = entry['cat2']\n        cat_bonus = 0.0\n\n        if cat2 == 'OPEN' and any(k in span_lower for k in ['open', '开路']):\n            cat_bonus = 0.2\n        elif cat2 == 'SHORT' and any(k in span_lower for k in ['short', '短路']):\n            cat_bonus = 0.2\n        elif cat2 == 'FIXTURE_CONTACT' and any(k in span_lower for k in ['fixture', '治具', 'contact', '接触', 'probe', '探针']):\n            cat_bonus = 0.25\n        elif cat2 == 'COLD_SOLDER' and any(k in span_lower for k in ['solder', '焊', 'cold', '虚', 'float', '浮']):\n            cat_bonus = 0.25\n        elif cat2 == 'FLASH_FAIL' and any(k in span_lower for k in ['flash', 'program', '烧录', 'write']):\n            cat_bonus = 0.25\n        elif cat2 == 'COMPONENT' and any(k in span_lower for k in ['component', '器件', 'ic', 'chip']):\n            cat_bonus = 0.2\n\n        # Label overlap\n        label_norm = self._normalize(entry['label'])\n        label_tokens = set(re.findall(r'\\b\\w+\\b|[\\u4e00-\\u9fff]+', label_norm))\n        span_tokens = set(re.findall(r'\\b\\w+\\b|[\\u4e00-\\u9fff]+', span_norm))\n\n        if label_tokens and span_tokens:\n            label_overlap = len(label_tokens & span_tokens) / len(label_tokens | span_tokens)\n        else:\n            label_overlap = 0.0\n\n        # Combine scores\n        # Keywords are most important\n        final_score = (keyword_score * 0.5) + (cat_bonus * 0.3) + (label_overlap * 0.2)\n\n        # Evidence\n        evidence = []\n        if keyword_matches > 0:\n            evidence.append(f\"kw:{keyword_matches}/{total_keywords}\")\n        if cat_bonus > 0.1:\n            evidence.append(f\"cat\")\n        if label_overlap > 0.15:\n            evidence.append(f\"label:{label_overlap:.2f}\")\n\n        return final_score, ', '.join(evidence) if evidence else \"weak\"\n\n    def _deterministic_pick(self, candidates: List[Tuple[str, float, str]], context: str) -> int:\n        \"\"\"Pick one of top candidates deterministically\"\"\"\n        if not candidates:\n            return -1\n\n        # Sort by score\n        sorted_cands = sorted(enumerate(candidates), key=lambda x: x[1][1], reverse=True)\n\n        best_idx, (best_code, best_score, _) = sorted_cands[0]\n\n        # Check for ties (within 0.07)\n        tied_idxs = [i for i, (code, score, _) in enumerate(candidates)\n                     if score >= best_score - 0.07]\n\n        if len(tied_idxs) == 1:\n            return best_idx\n\n        # Use hash for tiebreak\n        seed = int(hashlib.md5(context.encode()).hexdigest(), 16)\n        return tied_idxs[seed % len(tied_idxs)]\n\n    def normalize_record(self, row: Dict, product_id: str) -> Dict:\n        \"\"\"Normalize one record\"\"\"\n        record_id = row['record_id']\n        raw_text = row.get('raw_reason_text', '').strip()\n        station = row.get('station', '').strip()\n        engineer_id = row.get('engineer_id', '').strip()\n\n        if product_id not in self.codebooks:\n            return {\n                'record_id': record_id,\n                'product_id': product_id,\n                'station': station,\n                'engineer_id': engineer_id,\n                'raw_reason_text': raw_text,\n                'normalized': []\n            }\n\n        if not raw_text:\n            return {\n                'record_id': record_id,\n                'product_id': product_id,\n                'station': station,\n                'engineer_id': engineer_id,\n                'raw_reason_text': raw_text,\n                'normalized': []\n            }\n\n        segments = self._segment(raw_text)\n        entries = self.codebooks[product_id]\n\n        normalized = []\n\n        for seg_idx, span in enumerate(segments, 1):\n            segment_id = f\"{record_id}-S{seg_idx}\"\n\n            # Score all entries\n            candidates = []\n            for entry in entries:\n                score, evidence = self._score_match(span, entry, station)\n                if score > 0:\n                    candidates.append((entry['code'], score, evidence))\n\n            if not candidates:\n                # No match\n                seg_obj = {\n                    'segment_id': segment_id,\n                    'span_text': span,\n                    'pred_code': 'UNKNOWN',\n                    'pred_label': '',\n                    'confidence': 0.15,\n                    'rationale': 'no_match'\n                }\n            else:\n                # Pick best\n                best_idx = self._deterministic_pick(candidates, f\"{record_id}-{seg_idx}-{station}\")\n                code, score, evidence = candidates[best_idx]\n\n                # Decide on confidence and UNKNOWN\n                if score < 0.30:\n                    pred_code = 'UNKNOWN'\n                    pred_label = ''\n                    confidence = min(0.30, score + 0.12)\n                else:\n                    pred_code = code\n                    # Find label\n                    pred_label = next((e['label'] for e in entries if e['code'] == code), '')\n\n                    # Map score to confidence\n                    base_conf = 0.55 + (score * 0.40)\n\n                    # Reduce if second place is close\n                    if len(candidates) > 1:\n                        _, second_score, _ = candidates[1]\n                        if second_score >= score - 0.06:\n                            base_conf *= 0.85\n\n                    confidence = min(0.95, base_conf)\n\n                seg_obj = {\n                    'segment_id': segment_id,\n                    'span_text': span,\n                    'pred_code': pred_code,\n                    'pred_label': pred_label,\n                    'confidence': round(confidence, 4),\n                    'rationale': evidence\n                }\n\n            normalized.append(seg_obj)\n\n        return {\n            'record_id': record_id,\n            'product_id': product_id,\n            'station': station,\n            'engineer_id': engineer_id,\n            'raw_reason_text': raw_text,\n            'normalized': normalized\n        }\n\n    def process(self, log_path: str, output_path: str):\n        \"\"\"Process all logs\"\"\"\n        records = []\n\n        with open(log_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n            for idx, row in enumerate(reader):\n                if idx % 1000 == 0:\n                    print(f\"Processing {idx + 1}...\")\n\n                product = row.get('product_id', '').strip()\n                rec = self.normalize_record(row, product)\n                records.append(rec)\n\n        # Split on major punctuation\n        parts = re.split(r'[,，;；]', text)\n\n        segments = []\n        for part in parts:\n            part = part.strip()\n            if not part:\n                continue\n\n            # For longer parts, try \"/\" split but be conservative\n            if len(part) > 50 and ' / ' in part:\n                sub = re.split(r'\\s+/\\s+', part)\n                if len(sub) <= 3:  # Only split if reasonable\n                    segments.extend([p.strip() for p in sub if p.strip()])\n                else:\n                    segments.append(part)\n            else:\n                segments.append(part)\n\n        return segments if segments else [text]\n\n    def _score_match(self, span: str, entry: Dict, station: str) -> Tuple[float, str]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Score how well span matches entry.\n        Returns: (score 0-1, evidence_string)\n        \"\"\"\n        # Label overlap\n        label_norm = self._normalize(entry['label'])\n        label_tokens = set(re.findall(r'\\b\\w+\\b|[\\u4e00-\\u9fff]+', label_norm))\n        span_tokens = set(re.findall(r'\\b\\w+\\b|[\\u4e00-\\u9fff]+', span_norm))\n\n        if label_tokens and span_tokens:\n            label_overlap = len(label_tokens & span_tokens) / len(label_tokens | span_tokens)\n        else:\n            label_overlap = 0.0\n\n        # Sort by score\n        sorted_cands = sorted(enumerate(candidates), key=lambda x: x[1][1], reverse=True)\n\n        best_idx, (best_code, best_score, _) = sorted_cands[0]\n\n        # Check for ties (within 0.07)\n        tied_idxs = [i for i, (code, score, _) in enumerate(candidates)\n                     if score >= best_score - 0.07]\n\n        if len(tied_idxs) == 1:\n            return best_idx\n\n        # Use hash for tiebreak\n        seed = int(hashlib.md5(context.encode()).hexdigest(), 16)\n        return tied_idxs[seed % len(tied_idxs)]\n\n    def normalize_record(self, row: Dict, product_id: str) -> Dict:\n        \"\"\"Normalize one record\"\"\"\n        record_id = row['record_id']\n        raw_text = row.get('raw_reason_text', '').strip()\n        station = row.get('station', '').strip()\n        engineer_id = row.get('engineer_id', '').strip()\n\n        if product_id not in self.codebooks:\n            return {\n                'record_id': record_id,\n                'product_id': product_id,\n                'station': station,\n                'engineer_id': engineer_id,\n                'raw_reason_text': raw_text,\n                'normalized': []\n            }\n\n        if not raw_text:\n            return {\n                'record_id': record_id,\n                'product_id': product_id,\n                'station': station,\n                'engineer_id': engineer_id,\n                'raw_reason_text': raw_text,\n                'normalized': []\n            }\n\n        segments = self._segment(raw_text)\n        entries = self.codebooks[product_id]\n\n        normalized = []\n\n        for seg_idx, span in enumerate(segments, 1):\n            segment_id = f\"{record_id}-S{seg_idx}\"\n\n            # Score all entries\n            candidates = []\n            for entry in entries:\n                score, evidence = self._score_match(span, entry, station)\n                if score > 0:\n                    candidates.append((entry['code'], score, evidence))\n\n            if not candidates:\n                # No match\n                seg_obj = {\n                    'segment_id': segment_id,\n                    'span_text': span,\n                    'pred_code': 'UNKNOWN',\n                    'pred_label': '',\n                    'confidence': 0.15,\n                    'rationale': 'no_match'\n                }\n            else:\n                # Pick best\n                best_idx = self._deterministic_pick(candidates, f\"{record_id}-{seg_idx}-{station}\")\n                code, score, evidence = candidates[best_idx]\n\n                # Decide on confidence and UNKNOWN\n                if score < 0.30:\n                    pred_code = 'UNKNOWN'\n                    pred_label = ''\n                    confidence = min(0.30, score + 0.12)\n                else:\n                    pred_code = code\n                    # Find label\n                    pred_label = next((e['label'] for e in entries if e['code'] == code), '')\n\n                    # Map score to confidence\n                    base_conf = 0.55 + (score * 0.40)\n\n                    # Write output\n        with open(output_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            json.dump({'records': records}, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\n\n        print(f\"\\nProcessed {len(records)} records -> {output_path}\")\n\n        #
