product_id -> list of entries\n        self.stations_by_code = {}  # (product, code) -> set of stations\n\n    def load_codebooks(self, codebook_paths: Dict[str, str]):\n        \"\"\"Load all product codebooks\"\"\"\n        for product_id, path in codebook_paths.items():\n            entries = []\n            with open(path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n                reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n                for row in reader:\n                    stations = set(s.strip() for s in row['station_scope'].split(';'))\n                    keywords = [s.strip() for s in row['keywords_examples'].split(',')]\n\n                    entries.append({\n                        'product_id': row['product_id'],\n                        'code': row['code'],\n                        'label': row['standard_label'],\n                        'cat1': row['category_lv1'],\n                        'cat2': row['category_lv2'],\n                        'stations': stations,\n                        'keywords': keywords\n                    })\n\n            self.codebooks[product_id] = entries\n\n    def _normalize(self, text: str) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Normalize for comparison\"\"\"\n        return text.lower().strip()\n\n    def _segment(self, raw_text: str) -> List[str]:\n        \"\"\"Smart segmentation\"\"\"\n        if not raw_text or not raw_text.strip():\n            return []\n\n        text = raw_text.strip()\n\n        # Split on major punctuation\n        parts = re.split(r'[,，;；]', text)\n\n        segments = []\n        for part in parts:\n            part = part.strip()\n            if not part:\n                continue\n\n            # For longer parts, try \"/\" split but be conservative\n            if len(part) > 50 and ' / ' in part:\n                sub = re.split(r'\\s+/\\s+', part)\n                if len(sub) <= 3:  # Only split if reasonable\n                    segments.extend([p.strip() for p in sub if p.strip()])\n                else:\n                    segments.append(part)\n            else:\n                segments.append(part)\n\n        return segments if segments else [text]\n\n    def _score_match(self, span: str, entry: Dict, station: str) -> Tuple[float, str]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Score how well span matches entry.\n        Returns: (score 0-1, evidence_string)\n        \"\"\"\n        # Station check\n        if entry['stations'] and station not in entry['stations']:\n            return 0.0, \"station_mismatch\"\n\n        span_norm = self._normalize(span)\n        span_lower = span.lower()\n\n        # Count keyword matches\n        keyword_matches = 0\n        total_keywords = len(entry['keywords'])\n\n        for kw in entry['keywords']:\n            kw_norm = self._normalize(kw)\n            # Check if keyword is in span (normalized or original)\n            if kw_norm in span_norm or kw in span or kw.lower() in span_lower:\n                keyword_matches += 1\n\n        if total_keywords == 0:\n            keyword_score = 0.0\n        else:\n            keyword_score = keyword_matches / total_keywords\n\n        # Category matching (bonus)\n        cat2 = entry['cat2']\n        cat_bonus = 0.0\n\n        if cat2 == 'OPEN' and any(k in span_lower for k in ['open', '开路']):\n            cat_bonus = 0.2\n        elif cat2 == 'SHORT' and any(k in span_lower for k in ['short', '短路']):\n            cat_bonus = 0.2\n        elif cat2 == 'FIXTURE_CONTACT' and any(k in span_lower for k in ['fixture', '治具', 'contact', '接触', 'probe', '探针']):\n            cat_bonus = 0.25\n        elif cat2 == 'COLD_SOLDER' and any(k in span_lower for k in ['solder', '焊', 'cold', '虚', 'float', '浮']):\n            cat_bonus = 0.25\n        elif cat2 == 'FLASH_FAIL' and any(k in span_lower for k in ['flash', 'program', '烧录', 'write']):\n            cat_bonus = 0.25\n        elif cat2 == 'COMPONENT' and any(k in span_lower for k in ['component', '器件', 'ic', 'chip']):\n            cat_bonus = 0.2\n\n        # Label overlap\n        label_norm = self._normalize(entry['label'])\n        label_tokens = set(re.findall(r'\\b\\w+\\b|[\\u4e00-\\u9fff]+', label_norm))\n        span_tokens = set(re.findall(r'\\b\\w+\\b|[\\u4e00-\\u9fff]+', span_norm))\n\n        if label_tokens and span_tokens:\n            label_overlap = len(label_tokens & span_tokens) / len(label_tokens | span_tokens)\n        else:\n            label_overlap = 0.0\n\n        # Combine scores\n        # Keywords are most important\n        final_score = (keyword_score * 0.5) + (cat_bonus * 0.3) + (label_overlap * 0.2)\n\n        # Evidence\n        evidence = []\n        if keyword_matches > 0:\n            evidence.append(f\"kw:{keyword_matches}/{total_keywords}\")\n        if cat_bonus > 0.1:\n            evidence.append(f\"cat\")\n        if label_overlap > 0.15:\n            evidence.append(f\"label:{label_overlap:.2f}\")\n\n        return final_score, ', '.join(evidence) if evidence else \"weak\"\n\n    def _deterministic_pick(self, candidates: List[Tuple[str, float, str]], context: str) -> int:\n        \"\"\"Pick one of top candidates deterministically\"\"\"\n        if not candidates:\n            return -1\n\n        # Sort by score\n        sorted_cands = sorted(enumerate(candidates), key=lambda x: x[1][1], reverse=True)\n\n        best_idx, (best_code, best_score, _) = sorted_cands[0]\n\n        # Check for ties (within 0.07)\n        tied_idxs = [i for i, (code, score, _) in enumerate(candidates)\n                     if score >= best_score - 0.07]\n\n        if len(tied_idxs) == 1:\n            return best_idx\n\n        # Use hash for tiebreak\n        seed = int(hashlib.md5(context.encode()).hexdigest(), 16)\n        return tied_idxs[seed % len(tied_idxs)]\n\n    def normalize_record(self, row: Dict, product_id: str) -> Dict:\n        \"\"\"Normalize one record\"\"\"\n        record_id = row['record_id']\n        raw_text = row.get('raw_reason_text', '').strip()\n        station = row.get('station', '').strip()\n        engineer_id = row.get('engineer_id', '').strip()\n\n        if product_id not in self.codebooks:\n            return {\n                'record_id': record_id,\n                'product_id': product_id,\n                'station': station,\n                'engineer_id': engineer_id,\n                'raw_reason_text': raw_text,\n                'normalized': []\n            }\n\n        if not raw_text:\n            return {\n                'record_id': record_id,\n                'product_id': product_id,\n                'station': station,\n                'engineer_id': engineer_id,\n                'raw_reason_text': raw_text,\n                'normalized': []\n            }\n\n        segments = self._segment(raw_text)\n        entries = self.codebooks[product_id]\n\n        normalized = []\n\n        for seg_idx, span in enumerate(segments, 1):\n            segment_id = f\"{record_id}-S{seg_idx}\"\n\n            # Score all entries\n            candidates = []\n            for entry in entries:\n                score, evidence = self._score_match(span, entry, station)\n                if score > 0:\n                    candidates.append((entry['code'], score, evidence))\n\n            if not candidates:\n                # No match\n                seg_obj = {\n                    'segment_id': segment_id,\n                    'span_text': span,\n                    'pred_code': 'UNKNOWN',\n                    'pred_label': '',\n                    'confidence': 0.15,\n                    'rationale': 'no_match'\n                }\n            else:\n                # Pick best\n                best_idx = self._deterministic_pick(candidates, f\"{record_id}-{seg_idx}-{station}\")\n                code, score, evidence = candidates[best_idx]\n\n                # Decide on confidence and UNKNOWN\n                if score < 0.30:\n                    pred_code = 'UNKNOWN'\n                    pred_label = ''\n                    confidence = min(0.30, score + 0.12)\n                else:\n                    pred_code = code\n                    # Find label\n                    pred_label = next((e['label'] for e in entries if e['code'] == code), '')\n\n                    # Map score to confidence\n                    base_conf = 0.55 + (score * 0.40)\n\n                    # Write output\n        with open(output_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            json.dump({'records': records}, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\n\n        print(f\"\\nProcessed {len(records)} records -> {output_path}\")\n\n        # product_id -> list of entries\n        self.stations_by_code = {}  # (product, code) -> set of stations\n\n    def load_codebooks(self, codebook_paths: Dict[str, str]):\n        \"\"\"Load all product codebooks\"\"\"\n        for product_id, path in codebook_paths.items():\n            entries = []\n            with open(path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n                reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n                for row in reader:\n                    stations = set(s.strip() for s in row['station_scope'].split(';'))\n                    keywords = [s.strip() for s in row['keywords_examples'].split(',')]\n\n                    entries.append({\n                        'product_id': row['product_id'],\n                        'code': row['code'],\n                        'label': row['standard_label'],\n                        'cat1': row['category_lv1'],\n                        'cat2': row['category_lv2'],\n                        'stations': stations,\n                        'keywords': keywords\n                    })\n\n            self.codebooks[product_id] = entries\n\n    def _normalize(self, text: str) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Normalize for comparison\"\"\"\n        return text.lower().strip()\n\n    def _segment(self, raw_text: str) -> List[str]:\n        \"\"\"Smart segmentation\"\"\"\n        if not raw_text or not raw_text.strip():\n            return []\n\n        text = raw_text.strip()\n\n        # Split on major punctuation\n        parts = re.split(r'[,，;；]', text)\n\n        segments = []\n        for part in parts:\n            part = part.strip()\n            if not part:\n                continue\n\n            # For longer parts, try \"/\" split but be conservative\n            if len(part) > 50 and ' / ' in part:\n                sub = re.split(r'\\s+/\\s+', part)\n                if len(sub) <= 3:  # Only split if reasonable\n                    segments.extend([p.strip() for p in sub if p.strip()])\n                else:\n                    segments.append(part)\n            else:\n                segments.append(part)\n\n        return segments if segments else [text]\n\n    def _score_match(self, span: str, entry: Dict, station: str) -> Tuple[float, str]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Score how well span matches entry.\n        Returns: (score 0-1, evidence_string)\n        \"\"\"\n        # Station check\n        if entry['stations'] and station not in entry['stations']:\n            return 0.0, \"station_mismatch\"\n\n        span_norm = self._normalize(span)\n        span_lower = span.lower()\n\n        # Count keyword matches\n        keyword_matches = 0\n        total_keywords = len(entry['keywords'])\n\n        for kw in entry['keywords']:\n            kw_norm = self._normalize(kw)\n            # Check if keyword is in span (normalized or original)\n            if kw_norm in span_norm or kw in span or kw.lower() in span_lower:\n                keyword_matches += 1\n\n        if total_keywords == 0:\n            keyword_score = 0.0\n        else:\n            keyword_score = keyword_matches / total_keywords\n\n        # Category matching (bonus)\n        cat2 = entry['cat2']\n        cat_bonus = 0.0\n\n        if cat2 == 'OPEN' and any(k in span_lower for k in ['open', '开路']):\n            cat_bonus = 0.2\n        elif cat2 == 'SHORT' and any(k in span_lower for k in ['short', '短路']):\n            cat_bonus = 0.2\n        elif cat2 == 'FIXTURE_CONTACT' and any(k in span_lower for k in ['fixture', '治具', 'contact', '接触', 'probe', '探针']):\n            cat_bonus = 0.25\n        elif cat2 == 'COLD_SOLDER' and any(k in span_lower for k in ['solder', '焊', 'cold', '虚', 'float', '浮']):\n            cat_bonus = 0.25\n        elif cat2 == 'FLASH_FAIL' and any(k in span_lower for k in ['flash', 'program', '烧录', 'write']):\n            cat_bonus = 0.25\n        elif cat2 == 'COMPONENT' and any(k in span_lower for k in ['component', '器件', 'ic', 'chip']):\n            cat_bonus = 0.2\n\n        # Label overlap\n        label_norm = self._normalize(entry['label'])\n        label_tokens = set(re.findall(r'\\b\\w+\\b|[\\u4e00-\\u9fff]+', label_norm))\n        span_tokens = set(re.findall(r'\\b\\w+\\b|[\\u4e00-\\u9fff]+', span_norm))\n\n        if label_tokens and span_tokens:\n            label_overlap = len(label_tokens & span_tokens) / len(label_tokens | span_tokens)\n        else:\n            label_overlap = 0.0\n\n        # Combine scores\n        # Keywords are most important, category bonus is significant\n        final_score = (keyword_score * 0.5) + (cat_bonus * 0.4) + (label_overlap * 0.1)\n\n        # Evidence\n        evidence = []\n        if keyword_matches > 0:\n            evidence.append(f\"kw:{keyword_matches}/{total_keywords}\")\n        if cat_bonus > 0.1:\n            evidence.append(f\"cat\")\n        if label_overlap > 0.15:\n            evidence.append(f\"label:{label_overlap:.2f}\")\n\n        return final_score, ', '.join(evidence) if evidence else \"weak\"\n\n    def _deterministic_pick(self, candidates: List[Tuple[str, float, str]], context: str) -> int:\n        \"\"\"Pick one of top candidates deterministically\"\"\"\n        if not candidates:\n            return -1\n\n        # Sort by score\n        sorted_cands = sorted(enumerate(candidates), key=lambda x: x[1][1], reverse=True)\n\n        best_idx, (best_code, best_score, _) = sorted_cands[0]\n\n        # Check for ties (within 0.07)\n        tied_idxs = [i for i, (code, score, _) in enumerate(candidates)\n                     if score >= best_score - 0.07]\n\n        if len(tied_idxs) == 1:\n            return best_idx\n\n        # Use hash for tiebreak\n        seed = int(hashlib.md5(context.encode()).hexdigest(), 16)\n        return tied_idxs[seed % len(tied_idxs)]\n\n    def normalize_record(self, row: Dict, product_id: str) -> Dict:\n        \"\"\"Normalize one record\"\"\"\n        record_id = row['record_id']\n        raw_text = row.get('raw_reason_text', '').strip()\n        station = row.get('station', '').strip()\n        engineer_id = row.get('engineer_id', '').strip()\n\n        if product_id not in self.codebooks:\n            return {\n                'record_id': record_id,\n                'product_id': product_id,\n                'station': station,\n                'engineer_id': engineer_id,\n                'raw_reason_text': raw_text,\n                'normalized': []\n            }\n\n        if not raw_text:\n            return {\n                'record_id': record_id,\n                'product_id': product_id,\n                'station': station,\n                'engineer_id': engineer_id,\n                'raw_reason_text': raw_text,\n                'normalized': []\n            }\n\n        segments = self._segment(raw_text)\n        entries = self.codebooks[product_id]\n\n        normalized = []\n\n        for seg_idx, span in enumerate(segments, 1):\n            segment_id = f\"{record_id}-S{seg_idx}\"\n\n            # Score all entries\n            candidates = []\n            for entry in entries:\n                score, evidence = self._score_match(span, entry, station)\n                if score > 0:\n                    candidates.append((entry['code'], score, evidence))\n\n            if not candidates:\n                # No match\n                seg_obj = {\n                    'segment_id': segment_id,\n                    'span_text': span,\n                    'pred_code': 'UNKNOWN',\n                    'pred_label': '',\n                    'confidence': 0.15,\n                    'rationale': 'no_match'\n                }\n            else:\n                # Pick best\n                best_idx = self._deterministic_pick(candidates, f\"{record_id}-{seg_idx}-{station}\")\n                code, score, evidence = candidates[best_idx]\n\n                # Decide on confidence and UNKNOWN\n                if score < 0.30:\n                    pred_code = 'UNKNOWN'\n                    pred_label = ''\n                    confidence = min(0.30, score + 0.12)\n                else:\n                    pred_code = code\n                    # Find label\n                    pred_label = next((e['label'] for e in entries if e['code'] == code), '')\n\n                    # Map score to confidence\n                    base_conf = 0.55 + (score * 0.40)\n\n                    # Reduce if second place is close\n                    if len(candidates) > 1:\n                        _, second_score, _ = candidates[1]\n                        if second_score >= score - 0.06:\n                            base_conf *= 0.85\n\n                    confidence = min(0.95, base_conf)\n\n                seg_obj = {\n                    'segment_id': segment_id,\n                    'span_text': span,\n                    'pred_code': pred_code,\n                    'pred_label': pred_label,\n                    'confidence': round(confidence, 4),\n                    'rationale': evidence\n                }\n\n            normalized.append(seg_obj)\n\n        return {\n            'record_id': record_id,\n            'product_id': product_id,\n            'station': station,\n            'engineer_id': engineer_id,\n            'raw_reason_text': raw_text,\n            'normalized': normalized\n        }\n\n    def process(self, log_path: str, output_path: str):\n        \"\"\"Process all logs\"\"\"\n        records = []\n\n        with open(log_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n            for idx, row in enumerate(reader):\n                if idx % 1000 == 0:\n                    print(f\"Processing {idx + 1}...\")\n\n                product = row.get('product_id', '').strip()\n                rec = self.normalize_record(row, product)\n                records.append(rec)\n\n        #
